- Scene or process: Work produces compensation that continues as personal subsistence.
- Active motifs: worker's wage `ع م ل:B004/m01`; continuing stipend `ج ر ي:B006/m01`; livelihood and good condition `ط ع م:B004/m02`; earned activity `س ع ي:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:3 `عاملة` (`ع م ل`); 88:12 `جارية` (`ج ر ي`); 88:6 `طعام` (`ط ع م`); 88:9 `سعيها` (`س ع ي`)
- Synthesis: Labor, earned return, continuing payment, and livelihood form one compensation process.

#### Subchannel D. Levy, Assessed Share, and Account
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An authority assesses a fixed portion, collects it, and records the total.
- Active motifs: tribute or toll `ء ت ي:B008/m01`; assigned share `ن ص ب:B005/m01`; assessable base or threshold `ن ص ب:B006/m02`; apportionment among partners `س م ن:B008/m01`; counting and account `ح س ب:B001/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:1 `أتى` (`ء ت ي`); 88:3 and 88:19 forms of `نصب` (`ن ص ب`); 88:7 `يسمن` (`س م ن`); 88:26 `حسابهم` (`ح س ب`)
- Synthesis: Assessment, division, collection, and accounting define an institutional allocation mechanism.

#### Subchannel E. Discount, Redemption, and Manumission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A liability is reduced through payment or work until a person or asset is released.
- Active motifs: capital reduction or loss `و ض ع:B004/m01`; earning toward manumission `س ع ي:B005/m01`; spending or payment `ض ر ع:B009/m01`; entrusting a deposit `و ض ع:B010/m01`; restriction lifted `ء ل ي:B009/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:14 `موضوعة` (`و ض ع`); 88:9 `سعيها` (`س ع ي`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`); 88:17-20 `إلى` (`ء ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Payment, earned redemption, lowered balance, safekeeping, and release form one liability-settlement scene.

#### Subchannel F. Gift and Continuing Benefaction
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A donor bestows a benefit that may continue as support.
- Active motifs: bestowal `ء ت ي:B002/m01`; gift `ء ل ي:B012/m01`; assigned benefit `و ل ي:B013/m01`; continuing support `ج ر ي:B006/m02`; charitable spending `ض ر ع:B009/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:1 `أتى` (`ء ت ي`); 88:17-20 `إلى` (`ء ل ي`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`); 88:12 `جارية` (`ج ر ي`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`)
- Synthesis: Giving, assignment, continuation, and expenditure distinguish benefaction from reciprocal commerce.

### 22. Ritual Commitment and Response
- Semantic invariant: A person directs worship, sacrifice, blessing, or sworn obligation toward a sacred addressee and becomes answerable for the act.
- Surface relation: indirect; 88:2, 88:4, 88:9, 88:24
- Surprising reach: Prayer, animal sacrifice, rain invocation, gratitude, and oath expiation become variants of binding response rather than isolated ritual vocabulary.

#### Subchannel A. Worship and Sacred Orientation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshipper turns toward a deity, performs an obligatory rite, and occupies a consecrated place.
- Active motifs: deity and worship `ء ل ه:B001/m01`; obligatory prayer `ص ل ي:B001/m01`; place of worship `ص ل ي:B008/m01`; directed face `و ج ه:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:24 `الله` (`ء ل ه`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`); 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`)
- Synthesis: Sacred direction joins bodily orientation, prescribed action, and the place set apart for it.

#### Subchannel B. Altar and Sacrifice
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An erected altar or sacrificial stone receives livestock offered within a worship setting.
- Active motifs: sacrificial stone `ن ص ب:B002/m01`; worshipped deity `ء ل ه:B001/m02`; ritual place `ص ل ي:B008/m02`; sacrificial livestock `ن ع م:B005/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:3, 88:19 `ناصبة`, `نصبت` (`ن ص ب`); 88:24 `الله` (`ء ل ه`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`); 88:8 `ناعمة` (`ن ع م`)
- Synthesis: Erection, sacred location, divine address, and animal wealth converge on the material scene of sacrifice.

#### Subchannel C. Blessing, Rain Prayer, and Gratitude
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A community asks for water, receives beneficial provision, and answers it with blessing or gratitude.
- Active motifs: blessing and prayer `ص ل ي:B002/m01`; rain supplication `س ق ي:B006/m01`; good condition and blessing `ن ع م:B001/m01`; bestowed favors `ء ل ي:B006/m01`; concealment or denial of blessing `ك ف ر:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`); 88:5 `تسقى` (`س ق ي`); 88:8 `ناعمة` (`ن ع م`); 88:17-20 `إلى` (`ء ل ي`); 88:23 `كفر` (`ك ف ر`)
- Synthesis: The channel follows provision from petition through receipt to the contrasting responses of gratitude and denial.

#### Subchannel D. Oath and Expiation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A speaker invokes the divine name, assumes a sworn obligation, and must discharge or expiate it.
- Active motifs: oath `ء ل ي:B007/m01`; divine invocation `ء ل ه:B002/m01`; expiation `ك ف ر:B009/m01`; assigned pledge `و ل ي:B013/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`); 88:24 `الله` (`ء ل ه`); 88:23 `كفر` (`ك ف ر`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Invocation, commitment, assigned liability, and expiation form a complete cycle of sworn responsibility.

### 23. Contest, Coercion, and Impact
- Semantic invariant: Opposed agents seek advantage by matching, confronting, compelling, overcoming, or physically striking one another.
- Surface relation: indirect; 88:3, 88:9, 88:23-24
- Surprising reach: Athletic rivalry, alliance, forced compliance, victory, and weapon impact occupy successive intensities of one adversarial process.

#### Subchannel A. Rivalry and Race
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Competitors match pace, seek precedence, and establish who follows whom.
- Active motifs: racing effort `س ع ي:B008/m01`; matched rivalry `س م و:B007/m01`; runner-up or follower `ص ل ي:B007/m01`; attempt to overpower `ك ب ر:B011/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:9 `لسعيها` (`س ع ي`); 88:18 `السماء` (`س م و`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`); 88:24 `الأكبر` (`ك ب ر`)
- Synthesis: Striving becomes contest when effort is measured against another agent's pace and rank.

#### Subchannel B. Confrontation, Hostility, and Alliance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Opposing parties meet openly while allies align themselves with one side.
- Active motifs: confrontation `ء ر ض:B007/m01`; face-to-face encounter `و ج ه:B003/m01`; open hostility `ن ص ب:B008/m01`; feud `ن و ر:B007/m01`; alliance `و ل ي:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:20 `الأرض` (`ء ر ض`); 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`); 88:3, 88:19 `ناصبة`, `نصبت` (`ن ص ب`); 88:4 `نارا` (`ن و ر`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Spatial opposition develops into declared hostility, with allegiance determining the resulting sides.

#### Subchannel C. Coercion and Repulse
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One party forces another toward an act while resistance attempts to turn the pressure back.
- Active motifs: compulsion `ج ب ل:B010/m01`; forcing into disobedience `ك ف ر:B007/m01`; repulse `و ج ه:B014/m01`; decisive penetration or arrival `ء ت ي:B013/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:19 `الجبال` (`ج ب ل`); 88:23 `كفر` (`ك ف ر`); 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`); 88:1 `أتى` (`ء ت ي`)
- Synthesis: The channel is organized by directed pressure: its imposition, resisted reversal, and eventual breakthrough.

#### Subchannel D. Victory and Mastery
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A contestant rises over opposition, attains control, and leaves the defeated party unable to resist.
- Active motifs: domination `ع ل و:B004/m01`; attainment and mastery `و ل ي:B012/m01`; overcoming `ر ض و:B005/m01`; animal overpowering its rider `ض ر ع:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:10 `عالية` (`ع ل و`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`); 88:9 `راضية` (`ر ض و`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`)
- Synthesis: Height, possession, submission, and lost control supply complementary signs of decisive mastery.

#### Subchannel E. Weapon Preparation and Impact
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Implements are hardened, polished, raised, and brought down in cutting, crushing, or facial blows.
- Active motifs: sword polishing `ح د ث:B007/m01`; hard or strong implement `ذ ك ر:B002/m01`; lash or sword strike `غ ش و:B006/m01`; cutting stroke `س ط ر:B004/m01`; facial blow `و ج ه:B013/m01`; spear shaft `ع م ل:B009/m01`; raised anvil or tool `ع ل و:B009/m01`; grinding stone `ص ل ي:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:1 `حديث` (`ح د ث`); 88:21 `فذكر` (`ذ ك ر`); 88:1 `الغاشية` (`غ ش و`); 88:20 `سطحت` (`س ط ر`); 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`); 88:3 `عاملة` (`ع م ل`); 88:10 `عالية` (`ع ل و`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Material preparation and bodily impact form one operational chain from making an implement effective to landing the blow.

### 24. Capacity, Endurance, and Failure
- Semantic invariant: An agent's task is evaluated by the effort required, the burden that can be borne, and whether persistence reaches an effective result.
- Surface relation: direct; 88:3, 88:9, 88:22, 88:24
- Surprising reach: Labor, load-bearing, patience, courage, defective judgment, and readiness become different tests of practical capacity.

#### Subchannel A. Exertion and Earned Work
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A worker applies sustained effort, incurs fatigue, and earns the result of that labor.
- Active motifs: productive work `ع م ل:B007/m01`; striving `س ع ي:B002/m01`; fatiguing exertion `ء ل ي:B010/m01`; weariness `ن ص ب:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:3 `عاملة` (`ع م ل`); 88:9 `لسعيها` (`س ع ي`); 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`); 88:3 `ناصبة` (`ن ص ب`)
- Synthesis: Work is measured not only by motion but by sustained expenditure, fatigue, and the outcome credited to it.

#### Subchannel B. Capacity and Burden
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person or animal is tested by the quantity it can carry, consume, or otherwise sustain.
- Active motifs: ability and capacity `ء ل ي:B011/m01`; load-bearing `ء ب ل:B004/m01`; capacity to eat `ط ع م:B011/m01`; magnified burden `ك ب ر:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`); 88:17 `الإبل` (`ء ب ل`); 88:6 `طعام` (`ط ع م`); 88:24 `الأكبر` (`ك ب ر`)
- Synthesis: Capacity appears as a threshold at which intake, carriage, or obligation becomes too large for the bearer.

#### Subchannel C. Patience and Tolerance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An agent delays reaction, contains discomfort, and remains steady under prolonged strain.
- Active motifs: patient delay `ء ن ي:B001/m01`; tolerance and restraint `ل ي س:B006/m01`; bearing hardship `ء ل ي:B013/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:5 `آنية` (`ء ن ي`); 88:6, 88:22 `ليس`, `لست` (`ل ي س`); 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Endurance is represented as temporal extension joined to the refusal to break under pressure.

#### Subchannel D. Courage and Toughness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person meets danger with firmness of body and heart.
- Active motifs: courage `ل ي س:B004/m01`; hardness and strength `ذ ك ر:B002/m02`; heart made firm by polishing `ح د ث:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:6, 88:22 `ليس`, `لست` (`ل ي س`); 88:21 `فذكر`, `مذكر` (`ذ ك ر`); 88:1 `حديث` (`ح د ث`)
- Synthesis: Courage is not mere force; it is the maintained hardness and clarity needed to face a threat.

#### Subchannel E. Weak Judgment, Defect, and Failure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Poor judgment or malformed execution prevents an act from reaching its intended end.
- Active motifs: weak judgment `ل ي س:B007/m01`; physical weakness `ض ر ع:B003/m01`; defective placement or form `و ض ع:B012/m01`; inability or failure `ء ل ي:B008/m01`; futile act or utterance `ل غ و:B006/m01`; crooked line `س ط ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:6, 88:22 `ليس`, `لست` (`ل ي س`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`); 88:14 `موضوعة` (`و ض ع`); 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`); 88:11 `لاغية` (`ل غ و`); 88:20 `سطحت` (`س ط ر`)
- Synthesis: Failure can arise in the chooser, the instrument, the arrangement, or the act itself, but each variant blocks completion.

#### Subchannel F. Readiness and Effectiveness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A capable agent or well-formed instrument is positioned and made ready for decisive use.
- Active motifs: decisive execution `ء ت ي:B013/m02`; fitness for purpose `خ ل ق:B005/m02`; prepared orientation `و ج ه:B008/m01`; effective action on a field `ء ر ض:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:1 `أتى` (`ء ت ي`); 88:17 `خلقت` (`خ ل ق`); 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`); 88:20 `الأرض` (`ء ر ض`)
- Synthesis: Effectiveness joins intrinsic fitness to correct positioning and timely execution.

### 25. Moral Refusal and Consequence
- Semantic invariant: A person rejects rightful orientation through pride, disbelief, or disavowal and thereby enters a corresponding field of blame and punishment.
- Surface relation: direct; 88:2-4, 88:23-24
- Surprising reach: Inner self-magnification, denial of benefit, broken allegiance, grave offense, and social duplicity become stages or expressions of refusal.

#### Subchannel A. Arrogance and Self-Magnification
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person elevates the self, enlarges personal importance, and resists being placed under another claim.
- Active motifs: loftiness and arrogance `ع ل و:B003/m01`; self-magnification `ك ب ر:B006/m01`; pride swelling inwardly `ك ب ر:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:10 `عالية` (`ع ل و`); 88:24 `الأكبر` (`ك ب ر`)
- Synthesis: Moral elevation is distorted into self-elevation when greatness is claimed rather than acknowledged.

#### Subchannel B. Disbelief and Ingratitude
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A recipient covers or denies a recognized truth and the benefit that should have elicited gratitude.
- Active motifs: explicit disbelief `ك ف ر:B003/m01`; ingratitude or covering benefit `ك ف ر:B004/m02`; settled unbelief `ك ف ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:23 `وكفر` (`ك ف ر`)
- Synthesis: The same refusal can target truth as disbelief or target received good as ingratitude.

#### Subchannel C. Disavowal and Turning Away
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person renounces affiliation, reverses direction, and denies that a prior claim remains binding.
- Active motifs: renunciation `ك ف ر:B005/m01`; withdrawal of allegiance `و ل ي:B007/m01`; denial `ل ي س:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:23 `كفر` (`ك ف ر`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`); 88:6, 88:22 `ليس`, `لست` (`ل ي س`)
- Synthesis: Refusal becomes socially legible when inward denial is enacted as withdrawal from an allegiance or duty.

#### Subchannel D. Grave Offense, Punishment, and Toil
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A grave offense culminates in overwhelming punishment and exhausting subjection.
- Active motifs: grave offense `ك ب ر:B007/m01`; chastisement `ع ذ ب:B005/m01`; punitive toil `ن ص ب:B004/m02`; consequence coming upon its subject `ء ت ي:B011/m01`; overwhelming affliction `غ ش و:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:24 `الأكبر` (`ك ب ر`); 88:24 `فيعذبه`, `العذاب` (`ع ذ ب`); 88:3 `ناصبة` (`ن ص ب`); 88:1 `أتى` (`ء ت ي`); 88:1 `الغاشية` (`غ ش و`)
- Synthesis: Wrongdoing and consequence are joined by the arrival of a punishment that covers the offender and converts agency into toil.

#### Subchannel E. Duplicity and Social Shame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person presents incompatible faces, loses moral firmness, and becomes exposed to public reproach.
- Active motifs: two-faced duplicity `و ج ه:B015/m01`; shamelessness or weak protective jealousy `ل ي س:B008/m01`; loss of integrity or malformed conduct `و ض ع:B012/m02`; abusive or vain utterance `ل غ و:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:2, 88:8 `وجوه` (`و ج ه`); 88:6, 88:22 `ليس`, `لست` (`ل ي س`); 88:14 `موضوعة` (`و ض ع`); 88:11 `لاغية` (`ل غ و`)
- Synthesis: Social disgrace emerges where divided presentation, defective restraint, and injurious speech expose a failure of character.

### 26. Body Morphology and Sensory Anatomy
- Semantic invariant: Bodily parts are identified by their cavities, coverings, proportions, markings, and sensory functions.
- Surface relation: indirect; 88:2, 88:5-6, 88:8, 88:11
- Surprising reach: Eye and ear anatomy extends into brain, muscle, bone covering, body proportion, skin pattern, and facial marking.

#### Subchannel A. Eye, Ear, and Brain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sensory openings receive sight and sound while connected hollows and inner tissue support perception.
- Active motifs: eye or pupil `ن ظ ر:B008/m01`; ear as hearing organ `س م ع:B002/m02`; brain or head interior `س م ع:B016/m01`; eye socket or hollow `ع ي ن:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:17 `ينظرون` (`ن ظ ر`); 88:11 `تسمع` (`س م ع`); 88:5, 88:12 `عين` (`ع ي ن`)
- Synthesis: Perception is grounded in an anatomical route from exposed sensory opening to protected interior.

#### Subchannel B. Flesh, Muscle, and Bone Covering
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Soft tissue, muscle, membrane, and enclosing bone define layers of bodily structure.
- Active motifs: fleshy buttock `ء ل ي:B015/m01`; calf muscle `ح م ي:B009/m01`; periosteum or bony covering `ض ر ع:B012/m02`; chest or rib enclosure `ج ن ن:B016/m01`; thick-set body `ج ب ل:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:17-20, 88:25 `إلى`, `إلينا` (`ء ل ي`); 88:4 `حامية` (`ح م ي`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`); 88:10 `جنة` (`ج ن ن`); 88:19 `الجبال` (`ج ب ل`)
- Synthesis: The body appears as nested structural layers whose density and covering determine shape and strength.

#### Subchannel C. Proportion, Crease, and Marking
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body is recognized by relative dimensions, linear creases, colored variation, and distinctive facial marks.
- Active motifs: head-neck proportion `ك و ب:B006/m01`; palm or forehead lines `س ر ر:B009/m01`; variegated bodily marking `ح س ب:B009/m02`; white facial blaze `غ ش و:B007/m02`; thin or misshapen body `س م ع:B012/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:14 `أكواب` (`ك و ب`); 88:13 `سرر` (`س ر ر`); 88:26 `حسابهم` (`ح س ب`); 88:1 `الغاشية` (`غ ش و`); 88:11 `تسمع` (`س م ع`)
- Synthesis: Proportion and surface inscription operate together as a vocabulary for identifying bodily form.

### 27. Hunting and Wild Fauna
- Semantic invariant: Animals outside ordinary domestic use are pursued, trapped, classified, or distinguished by age and form.
- Surface relation: indirect; 88:6, 88:8, 88:11, 88:17-18
- Surprising reach: Hunting implements and blinds connect the camel scene to ostrich, quail, predator cubs, and separated young stock.

#### Subchannel A. Hunt, Trap, and Blind
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter seeks game, works an implement, and channels the animal toward a snare or concealed enclosure.
- Active motifs: hunting expedition or implement `ط ع م:B006/m01`; seeking game `س م و:B006/m01`; snare or trap `ص ل ي:B005/m01`; hunting blind or animal pen `ز ر ب:B001/m02`; working forelimb `ع م ل:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:6 `طعام` (`ط ع م`); 88:18 `السماء` (`س م و`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`); 88:16 `زرابي` (`ز ر ب`); 88:3 `عاملة` (`ع م ل`)
- Synthesis: Pursuit, concealment, channeling, and capture compose a single hunting operation.

#### Subchannel B. Ostrich and Quail
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Wild birds are distinguished by species, bodily form, and resemblance to familiar animal shapes.
- Active motifs: ostrich `ن ع م:B006/m01`; ostrich-shaped form `ن ع م:B007/m02`; quail `س م ن:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:8 `ناعمة` (`ن ع م`); 88:7 `يسمن` (`س م ن`)
- Synthesis: Species naming and shape comparison create a compact field of ground-dwelling game birds.

#### Subchannel C. Carnivore Cub
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A young predator is classified by species, age, and emerging male strength.
- Active motifs: wolf or hyena cub `س م ع:B010/m01`; maleness and strength `ذ ك ر:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:11 `تسمع` (`س م ع`); 88:21 `فذكر`, `مذكر` (`ذ ك ر`)
- Synthesis: The specialized animal term combines juvenile status with the anticipated force of the mature predator.

#### Subchannel D. Goat and Separated Young Stock
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Young animals are identified, divided from the main herd, and managed as a distinct group.
- Active motifs: young goat `س ط ر:B007/m01`; separated young livestock `و ل ي:B015/m02`; herd group `ء ب ل:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:20 `سطحت` (`س ط ر`); 88:23 `تولى` (`و ل ي`); 88:17 `الإبل` (`ء ب ل`)
- Synthesis: Age classification becomes a management practice when young stock are sorted from the larger herd.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. Chessboard Play
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Players arrange a bounded game position and move pieces within it.
- Active motifs: chessboard play or chess piece `ك و ب:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 88:14 `أكواب` (`ك و ب`)
- Synthesis: A specialized game sense forms a self-contained activity distinct from the vessel and body-shape channels.

### S2. Termite, Dry Wood, and Bundled Fuel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Dry timber is gathered for fuel while a ground-dwelling insect consumes the same material.
- Active motifs: termite or earth creature `ء ر ض:B010/m01`; dry tree wood `ج ب ل:B008/m01`; bundled firewood `ء ب ل:B005/m02`; ignition or kindling `ص ل ي:B004/m04`
- Ayah anchors: 88:20 `الأرض` (`ء ر ض`); 88:19 `الجبال` (`ج ب ل`); 88:17 `الإبل` (`ء ب ل`); 88:4 `تصلى` (`ص ل ي`)
- Synthesis: Wood joins insect habitat, gathered fuel, and combustion in a compact material cycle.

### S3. Truffle Beneath Sandy Soil
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A truffle forms beneath a sandy surface and is recognized or extracted through its outer rind and ground sign.
- Active motifs: truffle or rind in soil `س ر ر:B013/m01`; sandy ridge habitat `س ر ر:B015/m02`; productive earth `ء ر ض:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:13 `سرر` (`س ر ر`); 88:20 `الأرض` (`ء ر ض`)
- Synthesis: Hidden growth, protective surface, and sandy terrain make one specialized gathering scene.

### S4. Ceremonial Crowning and Marriage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A prominent bride is adorned and crowned as a marriage is publicly marked.
- Active motifs: ceremonial crown `ك ف ر:B015/m01`; marriage union `غ ن ي:B006/m02`; socially prominent woman `غ ن ي:B005/m03`; eye-catching adornment `ع ي ن:B016/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:23 `كفر` (`ك ف ر`); 88:7 `يغني` (`غ ن ي`); 88:5, 88:12 `عين` (`ع ي ن`)
- Synthesis: Status, ornament, and union converge in the visible rite that distinguishes the bride.

### S5. Rivalrous Sexual Commerce
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Competing solicitors seek patrons and attempt to match or surpass one another in a commercial sexual setting.
- Active motifs: prostitution or sexual commerce `س ع ي:B007/m01`; matched rivalry `س م و:B007/m02`; keeping pace or circulating `ج ر ي:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:9 `لسعيها` (`س ع ي`); 88:18 `السماء` (`س م و`); 88:12 `جارية` (`ج ر ي`)
- Synthesis: Solicitation becomes a competitive market activity through pursuit, comparison, and circulation.

### S6. Strong and Diluted Drink
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A drink is served in a cup and adjusted between concentrated strength and a thinned or cooled state.
- Active motifs: strong or heated drink `ح م ي:B008/m01`; thin or diluted drink `ض ر ع:B011/m02`; drinking cup `ك و ب:B005/m02`; cooling or tempering `س م ن:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 88:4 `حامية` (`ح م ي`); 88:6 `ضريع` (`ض ر ع`); 88:14 `أكواب` (`ك و ب`); 88:7 `يسمن` (`س م ن`)
- Synthesis: Vessel, concentration, dilution, and temperature define one bounded process of beverage preparation.
